    1→# BLUEPILL - Instant Matrix Launch\n     2→# Uses saved state from matrix_state.json, detects existing windows\n     3→\n     4→$matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n     5→$stateFile = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n     6→$shadersDir = \"$matrixDir\\shaders\"\n     7→$windowRegistryPath = \"$matrixDir\\window-registry.json\"\n     8→\n     9→# Check if state exists\n    10→if (-not (Test-Path $stateFile)) {\n    11→    Write-Host \"\"\n    12→    Write-Host \" ERROR: No saved state found.\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    13→    Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' first to set up your Matrix.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    14→    Write-Host \"\"\n    15→    exit 1\n    16→}\n    17→\n    18→# Load state\n    19→try {\n    20→    $state = Get-Content $stateFile -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json\n    21→    $slots = @($state.lastSlots)\n    22→    if ($slots.Count -eq 0) {\n    23→        Write-Host \"\"\n    24→        Write-Host \" ERROR: No slots in saved state.\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    25→        Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' to configure.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    26→        Write-Host \"\"\n    27→        exit 1\n    28→    }\n    29→} catch {\n    30→    Write-Host \"\"\n    31→    Write-Host \" ERROR: Could not read state: $_\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    32→    Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' to reconfigure.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    33→    Write-Host \"\"\n    34→    exit 1\n    35→}\n    36→\n    37→Write-Host \"\"\n    38→Write-Host \" BLUEPILL - Instant Launch\" -ForegroundColor Blue\n    39→Write-Host \" =========================\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    40→Write-Host \"\"\n    41→Write-Host \" Saved state: slots [$($slots -join ', ')]\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    42→\n    43→# --- WINDOW POSITIONING (P/Invoke) ---\n    44→Add-Type -TypeDefinition @\"\n    45→using System;\n    46→using System.Runtime.InteropServices;\n    47→using System.Collections.Generic;\n    48→using System.Text;\n    49→using System.Diagnostics;\n    50→\n    51→public class BluepillAPI {\n    52→    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n    53→\n    54→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    55→    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n    56→\n    57→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    58→    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n    59→\n    60→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    61→    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n    62→\n    63→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    64→    public static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int X, int Y, int cx, int cy, uint uFlags);\n    65→\n    66→    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    67→    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n    68→\n    69→    public const uint SWP_NOZORDER = 0x0004;\n    70→    public const uint SWP_SHOWWINDOW = 0x0040;\n    71→\n    72→    private static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> foundWindows;\n    73→\n    74→    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindAllTerminalWindows() {\n    75→        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n    76→        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n    77→            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n    78→                uint processId;\n    79→                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out processId);\n    80→                try {\n    81→                    var process = Process.GetProcessById((int)processId);\n    82→                    if (process.ProcessName.Equals(\"WindowsTerminal\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {\n    83→                        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n    84→                        GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n    85→                        var title = sb.ToString();\n    86→                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title)) {\n    87→                            foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n    88→                        }\n    89→                    }\n    90→                } catch { }\n    91→            }\n    92→            return true;\n    93→        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n    94→        return foundWindows;\n    95→    }\n    96→\n    97→    // Fast title-only search (no process lookup) for polling\n    98→    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindWindowsByPattern(string pattern) {\n    99→        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n   100→        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n   101→            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n   102→                var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n   103→                GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n   104→                var title = sb.ToString();\n   105→                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title) && System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(title, pattern)) {\n   106→                    foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n   107→                }\n   108→            }\n   109→            return true;\n   110→        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n   111→        return foundWindows;\n   112→    }\n   113→}\n   114→\"@ -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n   115→\n   116→# Import WindowLayoutEngine for centralized positioning\n   117→. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n   118→\n   119→Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms\n   120→Add-Type -AssemblyName UIAutomationClient\n   121→Add-Type -AssemblyName UIAutomationTypes\n   122→\n   123→function Get-ScreenDimensions {\n   124→    $screen = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.WorkingArea\n   125→    return @{\n   126→        Width = $screen.Width\n   127→        Height = $screen.Height\n   128→        Left = $screen.X\n   129→        Top = $screen.Y\n   130→    }\n   131→}\n   132→\n   133→function Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation($windowHandle) {\n   134→    try {\n   135→        $auto = [System.Windows.Automation.AutomationElement]\n   136→        $winElement = $auto::FromHandle($windowHandle)\n   137→        if (-not $winElement) { return $null }\n   138→\n   139→        $allCondition = [System.Windows.Automation.Condition]::TrueCondition\n   140→        $children = $winElement.FindAll([System.Windows.Automation.TreeScope]::Descendants, $allCondition)\n   141→\n   142→        foreach ($child in $children) {\n   143→            $childName = $child.Current.Name\n   144→            if ($childName -match \"^Matrix-(\\d+)$\") {\n   145→                return [int]$Matches[1]\n   146→            }\n   147→        }\n   148→    } catch { }\n   149→    return $null\n   150→}\n   151→\n   152→function Wait-ForMatrixWindow([string]$profileName, [int]$timeoutMs = 5000) {\n   153→    # Poll for window with title containing profileName\n   154→    # Returns $true if found, $false if timeout\n   155→    # Uses fast title-only search (no process lookup) for speed\n   156→    $pollInterval = 100\n   157→    $startTime = Get-Date\n   158→\n   159→    while ($true) {\n   160→        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollInterval\n   161→\n   162→        # Strict timeout check\n   163→        if (((Get-Date) - $startTime).TotalMilliseconds -ge $timeoutMs) {\n   164→            return $false\n   165→        }\n   166→\n   167→        # Fast check - just look for window title matching profile name\n   168→        $matches = [BluepillAPI]::FindWindowsByPattern($profileName)\n   169→        if ($matches.Count -gt 0) {\n   170→            return $true\n   171→        }\n   172→    }\n   173→}\n   174→\n   175→function Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill {\n   176→    # Returns array of @{Handle, Slot} for Matrix windows\n   177→    $windows = [BluepillAPI]::FindAllTerminalWindows()\n   178→    $result = @()\n   179→\n   180→    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n   181→        if ($win.Value -match \"Redpill\") { continue }\n   182→        $slot = Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation $win.Key\n   183→        if ($slot) {\n   184→            $result += @{\n   185→                Handle = $win.Key\n   186→                Slot = $slot\n   187→            }\n   188→        }\n   189→    }\n   190→\n   191→    return $result\n   192→}\n   193→\n   194→function Position-MatrixWindows {\n   195→    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n   196→\n   197→    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill\n   198→\n   199→    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) {\n   200→        Write-Host \"   No Matrix windows detected\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n   201→        return\n   202→    }\n   203→\n   204→    # Try to restore saved positions first (like Chrome restoring window positions)\n   205→    if (Restore-WindowPositions -WindowInfo $windowInfo) {\n   206→        Write-Host \"   Restored $($windowInfo.Count) windows to saved positions\" -ForegroundColor Green\n   207→        return\n   208→    }\n   209→\n   210→    # Fall back to layout engine if no saved positions\n   211→    $windowHandles = @{}\n   212→    foreach ($win in $windowInfo) {\n   213→        $windowHandles[\"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"] = @{ Handle = $win.Handle }\n   214→    }\n   215→\n   216→    $result = Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode 'Auto'\n   217→\n   218→    Write-Host \"   Positioned $($windowInfo.Count) windows using layout engine\" -ForegroundColor Green\n   219→}\n   220→\n   221→# Find which slots are already open\n   222→Write-Host \" Checking for existing windows...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n   223→$existingWindows = [BluepillAPI]::FindAllTerminalWindows()\n   224→$openSlots = @{}\n   225→\n   226→foreach ($win in $existingWindows) {\n   227→    if ($win.Value -match \"Redpill\") { continue }\n   228→    $slot = Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation $win.Key\n   229→    if ($slot) {\n   230→        $openSlots[$slot] = $true\n   231→        Write-Host \"   Slot $slot already open\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n   232→    }\n   233→}\n   234→\n   235→# Launch only slots that aren't BLUEPILL - Instant Matrix Launch\n# Uses saved state from matrix_state.json, detects existing windows\n\n$matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n$stateFile = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n$shadersDir = \"$matrixDir\\shaders\"\n$windowRegistryPath = \"$matrixDir\\window-registry.json\"\n\n# Check if state exists\nif (-not (Test-Path $stateFile)) {\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" ERROR: No saved state found.\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' first to set up your Matrix.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    exit 1\n}\n\n# Load state\ntry {\n    $state = Get-Content $stateFile -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json\n    $slots = @($state.lastSlots)\n    if ($slots.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Host \"\"\n        Write-Host \" ERROR: No slots in saved state.\" -ForegroundColor Red\n        Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' to configure.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        Write-Host \"\"\n        exit 1\n    }\n} catch {\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" ERROR: Could not read state: $_\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' to reconfigure.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    exit 1\n}\n\nWrite-Host \"\"\nWrite-Host \" BLUEPILL - Instant Launch\" -ForegroundColor Blue\nWrite-Host \" =========================\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\nWrite-Host \"\"\nWrite-Host \" Saved state: slots [$($slots -join ', ')]\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n# --- WINDOW POSITIONING (P/Invoke) ---\nAdd-Type -TypeDefinition @\"\nusing System;\nusing System.Runtime.InteropServices;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\n\npublic class BluepillAPI {\n    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int X, int Y, int cx, int cy, uint uFlags);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n\n    public const uint SWP_NOZORDER = 0x0004;\n    public const uint SWP_SHOWWINDOW = 0x0040;\n\n    private static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> foundWindows;\n\n    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindAllTerminalWindows() {\n        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n                uint processId;\n                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out processId);\n                try {\n                    var process = Process.GetProcessById((int)processId);\n                    if (process.ProcessName.Equals(\"WindowsTerminal\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {\n                        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n                        GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n                        var title = sb.ToString();\n                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title)) {\n                            foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n                        }\n                    }\n                } catch { }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n        return foundWindows;\n    }\n\n    // Fast title-only search (no process lookup) for polling\n    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindWindowsByPattern(string pattern) {\n        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n                var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n                GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n                var title = sb.ToString();\n                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title) && System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(title, pattern)) {\n                    foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n                }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n        return foundWindows;\n    }\n}\n\"@ -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n\n# Import WindowLayoutEngine for centralized positioning\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n\nAdd-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms\nAdd-Type -AssemblyName UIAutomationClient\nAdd-Type -AssemblyName UIAutomationTypes\n\nfunction Get-ScreenDimensions {\n    $screen = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.WorkingArea\n    return @{\n        Width = $screen.Width\n        Height = $screen.Height\n        Left = $screen.X\n        Top = $screen.Y\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation($windowHandle) {\n    try {\n        $auto = [System.Windows.Automation.AutomationElement]\n        $winElement = $auto::FromHandle($windowHandle)\n        if (-not $winElement) { return $null }\n\n        $allCondition = [System.Windows.Automation.Condition]::TrueCondition\n        $children = $winElement.FindAll([System.Windows.Automation.TreeScope]::Descendants, $allCondition)\n\n        foreach ($child in $children) {\n            $childName = $child.Current.Name\n            if ($childName -match \"^Matrix-(\\d+)$\") {\n                return [int]$Matches[1]\n            }\n        }\n    } catch { }\n    return $null\n}\n\nfunction Wait-ForMatrixWindow([string]$profileName, [int]$timeoutMs = 5000) {\n    # Poll for window with title containing profileName\n    # Returns $true if found, $false if timeout\n    # Uses fast title-only search (no process lookup) for speed\n    $pollInterval = 100\n    $startTime = Get-Date\n\n    while ($true) {\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollInterval\n\n        # Strict timeout check\n        if (((Get-Date) - $startTime).TotalMilliseconds -ge $timeoutMs) {\n            return $false\n        }\n\n        # Fast check - just look for window title matching profile name\n        $matches = [BluepillAPI]::FindWindowsByPattern($profileName)\n        if ($matches.Count -gt 0) {\n            return $true\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill {\n    # Returns array of @{Handle, Slot} for Matrix windows\n    $windows = [BluepillAPI]::FindAllTerminalWindows()\n    $result = @()\n\n    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n        if ($win.Value -match \"Redpill\") { continue }\n        $slot = Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation $win.Key\n        if ($slot) {\n            $result += @{\n                Handle = $win.Key\n                Slot = $slot\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $result\n}\n\nfunction Position-MatrixWindows {\n    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill\n\n    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Host \"   No Matrix windows detected\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        return\n    }\n\n    # Try to restore saved positions first (like Chrome restoring window positions)\n    if (Restore-WindowPositions -WindowInfo $windowInfo) {\n        Write-Host \"   Restored $($windowInfo.Count) windows to saved positions\" -ForegroundColor Green\n        return\n    }\n\n    # Fall back to layout engine if no saved positions\n    $windowHandles = @{}\n    foreach ($win in $windowInfo) {\n        $windowHandles[\"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"] = @{ Handle = $win.Handle }\n    }\n\n    $result = Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode 'Auto'\n\n    Write-Host \"   Positioned $($windowInfo.Count) windows using layout engine\" -ForegroundColor Green\n}\n\n# Find which slots are already open\nWrite-Host \" Checking for existing windows...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n$existingWindows = [BluepillAPI]::FindAllTerminalWindows()\n$openSlots = @{}\n\nforeach ($win in $existingWindows) {\n    if ($win.Value -match \"Redpill\") { continue }\n    $slot = Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation $win.Key\n    if ($slot) {\n        $openSlots[$slot] = $true\n        Write-Host \"   Slot $slot already open\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    }\n}\n\n# Launch only slots that aren't open\nWrite-Host \" Launching windows...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n\n$launched = 0\nforeach ($slot in $slots) {\n    $pname = \"Matrix-$slot\"\n\n    if ($openSlots.ContainsKey($slot)) {\n        Write-Host \"   $pname - already open, skipping\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n        continue\n    }\n\n    Write-Host \"   Waiting for $pname...\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray -NoNewline\n    Start-Process wt -ArgumentList \"-p `\"$pname`\"\"\n\n    if (Wait-ForMatrixWindow $pname) {\n        Write-Host \" OK\" -ForegroundColor Green\n    } else {\n        Write-Host \" TIMEOUT\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    }\n    $launched++\n}\n\nif ($launched -eq 0 -and $openSlots.Count -gt 0) {\n    Write-Host \"   All windows already open\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n}\n\nWrite-Host \"\"\nWrite-Host \" Positioning windows...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\nPosition-MatrixWindows\n\n# Start background monitor for drag-and-drop snap\n# Runs silently, auto-exits when Matrix windows close\n$monitorScript = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_monitor.ps1\"\nif (Test-Path $monitorScript) {\n    Start-Process powershell -ArgumentList \"-NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File `\"$monitorScript`\"\" -WindowStyle Hidden\n    Write-Host \" Window monitor started (drag-snap enabled)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n}\n\nWrite-Host \"\"\nWrite-Host \" THE MATRIX HAS YOU.\" -ForegroundColor Green\nWrite-Host \" Type 'redpill' to customize.\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\nWrite-Host \"\"\n","numLines":278,"startLine":1,"totalLines":278}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"861596e3-3cb3-42c1-b447-0980074555e4"}
