'\\{DENS\\}',$cfg.Dens `\n        -replace '\\{L1\\}',$cfg.L1 -replace '\\{L2\\}',$cfg.L2 -replace '\\{L3\\}',$cfg.L3\n    try {\n        [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($path, $content)\n        Write-Log \"Shader saved successfully: $path\" \"SAVE\"\n        return $true\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Log \"ERROR saving shader: $($_.Exception.Message)\" \"SAVE\"\n        Write-Host \"\"\n        Write-Host \" Error saving shader: $($_.Exception.Message)\" -ForegroundColor Red\n        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n        return $false\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Load-TerminalEffects($slot) {\n    # Default values (degraded mode if JSON fails)\n    $script:transparency = $false\n    $script:opacity = 100\n\n    try {\n        $content = Get-Content $wtSettingsPath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n        $settings = $content | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n        $profile = $settings.profiles.list | Where-Object { $_.name -eq \"Matrix-$slot\" }\n\n        if ($profile) {\n            # Check for true transparency (opacity setting, integer 0-100)\n            if ($null -ne $profile.opacity -and $profile.opacity -lt 100) {\n                $script:transparency = $true\n                $script:opacity = [int]$profile.opacity\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    catch [System.IO.IOException] {\n        Write-Host \" Warning: Cannot read settings.json (file locked)\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        Write-Host \" Using default transparency settings\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500\n    }\n    catch [System.ArgumentException] {\n        Write-Host \" Warning: settings.json is malformed\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        Write-Host \" Using default transparency settings\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Host \" Warning: Could not load terminal settings: $($_.Exception.Message)\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        Write-Host \" Using default transparency settings\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Adj($p, $d, $mn, $mx) {\n    $v = [float]$s[$p] + $d\n    if ($v -ge $mn -and $v -le $mx) {\n        $s[$p] = $v.ToString(\"N1\")\n        $script:dirty = $true\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Bar($val, $min, $max, $width) {\n    $pct = ([float]$val - $min) / ($max - $min)\n    $filled = [int]($pct * $width)\n    $empty = $width - $filled\n    \"$([char]27)[32m$('=' * $filled)$([char]27)[90m$('-' * $empty)$([char]27)[0m\"\n}\n\n# --- WINDOW POSITIONING & TRANSPARENCY (P/Invoke) ---\nAdd-Type -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue -TypeDefinition @\"\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Runtime.InteropServices;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\n\npublic class WindowAPI {\n    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int X, int Y, int cx, int cy, uint uFlags);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT lpRect);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern int GetWindowLong(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern int SetWindowLong(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex, int dwNewLong);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern bool SetLayeredWindowAttributes(IntPtr hWnd, uint crKey, byte bAlpha, uint dwFlags);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", SetLastError = true)]\n    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr hWnd);\n\n    public const int SW_RESTORE = 9;\n\n    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]\n    public struct RECT {\n        public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom;\n    }\n\n    public const uint SWP_NOZORDER = 0x0004;\n    public const uint SWP_NOACTIVATE = 0x0010;\n    public const int GWL_EXSTYLE = -20;\n    public const int WS_EX_LAYERED = 0x80000;\n    public const uint LWA_ALPHA = 0x2;\n\n    private static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> foundWindows;\n\n    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindWindowsByPattern(string pattern) {\n        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n                var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n                GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n                var title = sb.ToString();\n                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title) && System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(title, pattern)) {\n                    foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n                }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n        return foundWindows;\n    }\n\n    // Find ALL Windows Terminal windows (by process name) except those matching excludePattern\n    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindAllTerminalWindows(string excludePattern) {\n        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n                uint processId;\n                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out processId);\n                try {\n                    var process = Process.GetProcessById((int)processId);\n                    // Check if this is a Windows Terminal window\n                    if (process.ProcessName.Equals(\"WindowsTerminal\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {\n                        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n                        GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n                        var title = sb.ToString();\n                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title)) {\n                            // Exclude windows matching the pattern (e.g., \"Redpill\")\n                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(excludePattern) ||\n                                !System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(title, excludePattern)) {\n                                foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                } catch { }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n        return foundWindows;\n    }\n}\n\"@\n\nAdd-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms\n\n# Import Window Layout Engine\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n\nfunction Get-ScreenDimensions {\n    $screen = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.WorkingArea\n    return @{\n        Width = $screen.Width\n        Height = $screen.Height\n        X = $screen.X\n        Y = $screen.Y\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Position-MatrixWindows {\n    # Position ALL open Windows Terminal windows (except Redpill) using the Window Layout Engine\n    # Supports Pillars (vertical columns) and Quads (2x2 grid) layout modes\n    Write-Log \"Positioning windows via Layout Engine...\" \"POSITION\"\n    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Log \"No windows to position\" \"POSITION\"\n        return\n    }\n\n    # Build hashtable for Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout: Key = shader name, Value = @{Handle}\n    $windowHandles = @{}\n    foreach ($win in $windowInfo) {\n        $windowHandles[\"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"] = @{ Handle = $win.Handle }\n    }\n\n    # Get layout configuration and invoke the layout engine\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    $mode = if ($config.Mode) { $config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n    Write-Log \"Layout mode: $mode, Windows: $($windowInfo.Count)\" \"POSITION\"\n\n    try {\n        Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode $mode\n        Write-Log \"Layout applied successfully\" \"POSITION\"\n\n        # Update position tracking after repositioning\n        $handleArray = @($windowInfo | ForEach-Object { $_.Handle })\n        if ($handleArray.Count -gt 0) {\n            Update-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handleArray\n        }\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Log \"ERROR applying layout: $($_.Exception.Message)\" \"POSITION\"\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Get-MatrixWindows {\n    # Find ALL Windows Terminal windows EXCEPT the Redpill control panel\n    # These are all \"Matrix windows\" because they run with shader effects\n    $windows = [WindowAPI]::FindAllTerminalWindows(\"Redpill\")\n    return $windows\n}\n\nfunction Get-WindowShaderMapping {\n    # Load window registry (handle -> shader file mapping)\n    $registry = @{}\n    if (Test-Path $windowRegistryPath) {\n        try {\n            $content = Get-Content $windowRegistryPath -Raw\n            $data = $content | ConvertFrom-Json\n            # Convert PSObject to hashtable\n            $data.PSObject.Properties | ForEach-Object { $registry[$_.Name] = $_.Value }\n        } catch { }\n    }\n    return $registry\n}\n\nfunction Clean-WindowRegistry {\n    # Remove stale entries for windows that no longer exist\n    if (-not (Test-Path $windowRegistryPath)) { return }\n\n    try {\n        $registry = Get-WindowShaderMapping\n        $currentWindows = Get-MatrixWindows\n        $validHandles = $currentWindows | ForEach-Object { $_.Key.ToString() }\n\n        $cleanedRegistry = @{}\n        foreach ($key in $registry.Keys) {\n            if ($key -in $validHandles) {\n                $cleanedRegistry[$key] = $registry[$key]\n            }\n        }\n\n        $cleanedRegistry | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content $windowRegistryPath -Encoding UTF8\n    } catch { }\n}\n\nfunction Populate-WindowRegistry {\n    # Assign shaders to current windows that aren't in registry yet\n    # Uses title matching, profile detection, then sequential assignment\n    $windows = Get-MatrixWindows\n    $registry = Get-WindowShaderMapping\n    $usedSlots = @{}\n\n    # Track which slots are already used\n    foreach ($key in $registry.Keys) {\n        if ($registry[$key] -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\\.hlsl\") {\n            $usedSlots[[int]$Matches[1]] = $true\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Also track slots from windows with Matrix-N titles\n    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n        if ($win.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n            $usedSlots[[int]$Matches[1]] = $true\n        }\n    }\n\n    $nextSlot = 1\n    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n        $handleKey = $win.Key.ToString()\n\n        # Skip if already in registry\n        if ($registry.ContainsKey($handleKey)) { continue }\n\n        # Try to determine slot from title\n        if ($win.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n            $slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n        }\n        # Try to determine from profile settings (only if slot not already used)\n        else {\n            $profileSlot = Get-SlotFromSettings $win.Value\n            if ($profileSlot -and -not $usedSlots.ContainsKey($profileSlot)) {\n                $slot = $profileSlot\n            }\n        }\n\n        # If still no slot, assign next available\n        if (-not $slot) {\n            while ($usedSlots.ContainsKey($nextSlot)) { $nextSlot++ }\n            $slot = $nextSlot\n            $nextSlot++\n        }\n\n        $registry[$handleKey] = \"Matrix-$slot.hlsl\"\n        $usedSlots[$slot] = $true\n    }\n\n    # Save updated registry\n    try {\n        $registry | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content $windowRegistryPath -Encoding UTF8\n    } catch { }\n}\n\nfunction Save-CurrentState {\n    # Save current open slots to matrix_state.json for Blue Pill to use\n    $stateFile = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n\n    $state = @{\n        lastSlots = $openSlots\n        lastSaved = (Get-Date).ToString(\"o\")\n    }\n\n    try {\n        $state | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content $stateFile -Encoding UTF8\n    } catch { }\n}\n\nfunction Get-OpenMatrixSlots {\n    # Extract slot numbers from windows using registry + title fallback\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n    return $windowInfo | ForEach-Object { $_.Slot } | Sort-Object -Unique\n}\n\nfunction Get-SlotFromSettings($windowTitle) {\n    # Determine shader slot by checking profile settings\n    # Returns slot number or $null if can't determine\n    try {\n        $content = Get-Content $wtSettingsPath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n        $settings = $content | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        # Check if window title contains any profile name with a shader override\n        foreach ($profile in $settings.profiles.list) {\n            if ($profile.name -and $windowTitle -like \"*$($profile.name)*\") {\n                if ($profile.'experimental.pixelShaderPath' -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\\.hlsl\") {\n                    return [int]$Matches[1]\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        # Fallback to profiles.defaults\n        $defaultShader = $settings.profiles.defaults.'experimental.pixelShaderPath'\n        if ($defaultShader -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\\.hlsl\") {\n            return [int]$Matches[1]\n        }\n    } catch { }\n    return $null\n}\n\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowInfo {\n    # Returns array of @{Handle, Title, Slot, ShaderPath} for Matrix windows ONLY\n    # Uses title matching to detect Matrix-N windows\n    Write-Log \"Detecting Matrix windows...\" \"DETECT\"\n    $windows = Get-MatrixWindows\n    Write-Log \"Found $($windows.Count) terminal windows\" \"DETECT\"\n    $result = @()\n\n    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n        $slot = $null\n        $shaderFile = $null\n\n        # Title matching (Matrix-N in title)\n        if ($win.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n            $slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n            $shaderFile = \"Matrix-$slot.hlsl\"\n            Write-Log \"  Title match: handle=$($win.Key) -> Slot $slot\" \"DETECT\"\n        }\n        # No Matrix profile detected - skip this window\n        else {\n            Write-Log \"  Skipping non-Matrix window: handle=$($win.Key) title='$($win.Value)'\" \"DETECT\"\n            continue\n        }\n\n        $result += @{\n            Handle = $win.Key\n            Title = $win.Value\n            Slot = $slot\n            ShaderFile = $shaderFile\n            ShaderPath = \"$shadersDir\\$shaderFile\"\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Update registry with detected mappings\n    $registry = @{}\n    foreach ($w in $result) {\n        $registry[$w.Handle.ToString()] = $w.ShaderFile\n    }\n    try {\n        $registry | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content $windowRegistryPath -Encoding UTF8\n    } catch { }\n\n    $sorted = $result | Sort-Object { $_.Slot }\n    Write-Log \"Detected $($sorted.Count) Matrix windows\" \"DETECT\"\n    foreach ($w in $sorted) {\n        Write-Log \"  Slot $($w.Slot): handle=$($w.Handle) title='$($w.Title)'\" \"DETECT\"\n    }\n    return $sorted\n}\n\nfunction Apply-WindowTransparency {\n    # Apply transparency via Windows Terminal profile settings (background only, not text/effects)\n    # This modifies settings.json and Windows Terminal hot-reloads\n    try {\n        $content = Get-Content $wtSettingsPath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n        $settings = $content | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        # Apply to profiles.defaults (affects windows using default profile)\n        if ($script:transparency) {\n            $settings.profiles.defaults | Add-Member -NotePropertyName 'opacity' -NotePropertyValue $script:opacity -Force\n        } else {\n            $settings.profiles.defaults.PSObject.Properties.Remove('opacity')\n        }\n\n        # Apply to ALL Matrix-N profiles\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $settings.profiles.list.Count; $i++) {\n            $profileName = $settings.profiles.list[$i].name\n            if ($profileName -match \"^Matrix-\\d+$\") {\n                if ($script:transparency) {\n                    $settings.profiles.list[$i] | Add-Member -NotePropertyName 'opacity' -NotePropertyValue $script:opacity -Force\n                } else {\n                    $settings.profiles.list[$i].PSObject.Properties.Remove('opacity')\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        # Safe atomic write\n        $json = $settings | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10\n        $tempPath = \"$wtSettingsPath.tmp\"\n        [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($tempPath, $json, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)\n        Move-Item $tempPath $wtSettingsPath -Force -ErrorAction Stop\n    }\n    catch { }\n}\n\nfunction Wait-ForMatrixWindow([string]$profileName, [int]$timeoutMs = 5000) {\n    # Poll for window with title containing profileName (e.g., \"Matrix-1\")\n    # Returns $true if found, $false if timeout\n    # Uses fast title-only search (no process lookup) for speed\n    $pollInterval = 100\n    $startTime = Get-Date\n\n    while ($true) {\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollInterval\n\n        # Strict timeout check\n        if (((Get-Date) - $startTime).TotalMilliseconds -ge $timeoutMs) {\n            return $false\n        }\n\n        # Fast check - just look for window title matching profile name\n        $matches = [WindowAPI]::FindWindowsByPattern($profileName)\n        if ($matches.Count -gt 0) {\n            return $true\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction Launch-MatrixWindows([int]$count) {\n    Write-Log \"Launch requested: count=$count\" \"LAUNCH\"\n    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n    Write-Log \"Existing slots: $($existingSlots -join ',') Open: $($openSlots -join ',')\" \"LAUNCH\"\n\n    # Find available slots (exist but not currently open)\n    $availableSlots = $existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }\n\n    if ($availableSlots.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Log \"No available slots to launch\" \"LAUNCH\"\n        Write-Host \"\"\n        Write-Host \" All Matrix windows are already open!\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n        return\n    }\n\n    $numWindows = [Math]::Min($count, $availableSlots.Count)\n    $slotsToLaunch = $availableSlots | Select-Object -First $numWindows\n    Write-Log \"Launching slots: $($slotsToLaunch -join ',')\" \"LAUNCH\"\n\n    # Save shaders for slots we're launching\n    foreach ($slot in $slotsToLaunch) {\n        $cfg = Load-Shader $slot\n        Save-Shader $slot $cfg\n    }\n\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" Launching $numWindows Matrix window(s)...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    Write-Host \" Open: [$($openSlots -join ', ')] | Launching: [$($slotsToLaunch -join ', ')]\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n    foreach ($slot in $slotsToLaunch) {\n        $pname = \"Matrix-$slot\"\n        Write-Host \"   Waiting for $pname...\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray -NoNewline\n        Start-Process wt -ArgumentList \"-p `\"$pname`\"\"\n\n        if (Wait-ForMatrixWindow $pname) {\n            Write-Log \"Window $pname launched successfully\" \"LAUNCH\"\n            Write-Host \" OK\" -ForegroundColor Green\n        } else {\n            Write-Log \"Window $pname TIMEOUT after 5s\" \"LAUNCH\"\n            Write-Host \" TIMEOUT (5s)\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Position ALL open Matrix windows (existing + new)\n    Write-Host \" Positioning windows...\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    Position-MatrixWindows\n\n    # Start background monitor for drag-snap (if not already running)\n    $monitorScript = \"$PSScriptRoot\\matrix_monitor.ps1\"\n    if (Test-Path $monitorScript) {\n        Start-Process powershell -ArgumentList \"-NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File `\"$monitorScript`\"\" -WindowStyle Hidden\n    }\n\n    Write-Host \" THE MATRIX HAS YOU.\" -ForegroundColor Green\n    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n}\n\nfunction UI {\n    Clear-Host\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo  # Get ALL Windows Terminal windows except Redpill\n    $dirtyMark = if ($dirty) { \"*\" } else { \" \" }\n\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" RED PILL$dirtyMark- Tab $currentSlot\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Tab selector - shows ALL Windows Terminal windows with shader effects\n    Write-Host \" TABS: \" -NoNewline\n    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Host \"(no Matrix windows detected)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray -NoNewline\n    } else {\n        foreach ($winInfo in $windowInfo) {\n            $slot = $winInfo.Slot\n            $title = $winInfo.Title\n            # For Matrix-N windows, load their shader config. For others, use defaults\n            if ($slot -lt 100) {\n                $cfg = Load-Shader $slot\n                $displayName = \"$slot\"\n            } else {\n                $cfg = $defaults.Clone()\n                # Abbreviate long titles (first 8 chars)\n                if ($title.Length -gt 8) {\n                    $displayName = $title.Substring(0, 8) + \"..\"\n                } else {\n                    $displayName = $title\n                }\n            }\n            if ($slot -eq $currentSlot) {\n                Write-Host \"[$displayName]$(Swatch $cfg.R $cfg.G $cfg.B 1)\" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor Yellow\n            } else {\n                Write-Host \" $displayName $(Swatch $cfg.R $cfg.G $cfg.B 1)\" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n            }\n            Write-Host \" \" -NoNewline\n        }\n    }\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" [TAB] next tab\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Color section\n    Write-Host \" COLOR PRESETS\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [1]$(Swatch 0 1 0.3 1)Green [2]$(Swatch 0 0.6 1 1)Cyan [3]$(Swatch 1 0.1 0.1 1)Red [4]$(Swatch 0.7 0 1 1)Purple [5]$(Swatch 1 0.7 0 1)Gold [6]$(Swatch 0 0.9 0.9 1)Teal\"\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Current color with sliders\n    Write-Host \" CURRENT $(Swatch $s.R $s.G $s.B 3)\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [Q/W] Red   $($s.R.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.R 0 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [A/S] Green $($s.G.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.G 0 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [Z/X] Blue  $($s.B.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.B 0 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Effects\n    Write-Host \" RAIN EFFECTS\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [E/R] Speed   $($s.Speed.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Speed 0.1 3 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [D/F] Glow    $($s.Glow.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Glow 0.2 3 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [C/V] Width   $($s.Width.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Width 6 20 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [T/Y] Trail   $($s.Trail.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Trail 4 15 15)\"\n    Write-Host \" [G/H] Density $($s.Dens.PadLeft(4)) $(Bar $s.Dens 0.2 1 15)\"\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Layers\n    $l1 = if($s.L1 -eq \"1.0\"){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $l2 = if($s.L2 -eq \"1.0\"){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $l3 = if($s.L3 -eq \"1.0\"){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $l1c = if($s.L1 -eq \"1.0\"){\"Green\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    $l2c = if($s.L2 -eq \"1.0\"){\"Green\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    $l3c = if($s.L3 -eq \"1.0\"){\"Green\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" LAYERS\" -ForegroundColor White\n    Write-Host \" [7] Far: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $l1 -ForegroundColor $l1c -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  [8] Mid: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $l2 -ForegroundColor $l2c -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  [9] Near: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $l3 -ForegroundColor $l3c\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Terminal Effects (transparency and layout)\n    Write-Host \" WINDOW EFFECTS\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n    $transStatus = if($transparency){\"ON \"}else{\"off\"}\n    $transColor = if($transparency){\"Cyan\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" [B] Transparency:  \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $transStatus -ForegroundColor $transColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  (toggles & applies)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    if ($transparency) {\n        Write-Host \" [K/l] Opacity:     $($opacity.ToString().PadLeft(3))% $(Bar $opacity 0 100 15)\"\n    }\n    # Layout mode display\n    $layoutConfig = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    $layoutMode = if ($layoutConfig.Mode) { $layoutConfig.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n    $layoutColor = if ($layoutMode -eq 'Pillars') { \"Yellow\" } else { \"Magenta\" }\n    Write-Host \" [Shift+L] Layout:  \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $layoutMode -ForegroundColor $layoutColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"  (Pillars=columns, Quads=2x2)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n    # Windows on Primary display\n    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n        $windowsOnPrimary = $layoutConfig.WindowsOnPrimary\n        if ($null -eq $windowsOnPrimary -or $windowsOnPrimary -lt 0) {\n            $primaryDisplay = \"Auto\"\n            $primaryColor = \"Cyan\"\n        } else {\n            $primaryDisplay = \"$windowsOnPrimary\"\n            $primaryColor = \"Yellow\"\n        }\n        Write-Host \" [</>] Primary:     \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $primaryDisplay -ForegroundColor $primaryColor -NoNewline\n        Write-Host \"  (windows on primary monitor)  [)] Auto\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    }\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Launch section\n    Write-Host \" LAUNCH\" -ForegroundColor Magenta\n    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n    $availableSlots = $existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }\n    $availableCount = $availableSlots.Count\n\n    # Show open/available status\n    $openStr = if ($openSlots.Count -gt 0) { $openSlots -join ',' } else { \"none\" }\n    $availStr = if ($availableCount -gt 0) { $availableSlots -join ',' } else { \"none\" }\n    Write-Host \" Open: \" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host $openStr -NoNewline -ForegroundColor Green\n    Write-Host \" | Available: \" -NoNewline -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host $availStr -ForegroundColor Cyan\n\n    $launchStatus = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"$launchCount window(s)\"}else{\"disabled\"}\n    $launchColor = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"Magenta\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" [-/+] Count: \" -NoNewline; Write-Host $launchStatus -ForegroundColor $launchColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \" (max: $availableCount)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host \"\"\n\n    # Footer\n    $enterAction = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"[ENTER] Launch $launchCount window(s)\"}else{\"[ENTER] (set count first)\"}\n    $enterColor = if($launchCount -gt 0){\"Yellow\"}else{\"DarkGray\"}\n    Write-Host \" $enterAction  \" -ForegroundColor $enterColor -NoNewline\n    Write-Host \"[P] Save shader\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    Write-Host \" [0] Reset  [ESC] Quit\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" Shader changes apply automatically when saved (hot-reload)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n}\n\n# Check for existing shaders\n$existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\nif ($existingSlots.Count -eq 0) {\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    Write-Host \" No Matrix tabs found.\" -ForegroundColor Red\n    Write-Host \" Run 'wakeupneo' first to create some.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n    Write-Host \"\"\n    exit 1\n}\n\n# Clean stale window registry entries and populate with current windows\nClean-WindowRegistry\nPopulate-WindowRegistry\n\n# Load first OPEN slot (or first existing if none open)\n$openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\nif ($openSlots.Count -gt 0) {\n    $currentSlot = $openSlots[0]\n} else {\n    $currentSlot = $existingSlots[0]  # Fallback if no windows open yet\n}\n$s = Load-Shader $currentSlot\nLoad-TerminalEffects $currentSlot\n\n# Drag detection is handled by matrix_monitor.ps1 background process\n# Redpill uses simple blocking input for responsive UI\n\n[Console]::CursorVisible = $false\ntry {\n    while ($true) {\n        UI\n        $key = $Host.UI.RawUI.ReadKey(\"NoEcho,IncludeKeyDown\")\n        $k = $key.Character\n        $vk = $key.VirtualKeyCode\n\n        # Tab key (VK 9) to switch between OPEN windows only\n        if ($vk -eq 9) {\n            # Auto-save before switching tabs\n            if ($dirty) {\n                Save-Shader $currentSlot $s\n                $dirty = $false\n            }\n            $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n            if ($openSlots.Count -gt 0) {\n                $idx = [array]::IndexOf($openSlots, $currentSlot)\n                if ($idx -lt 0) { $idx = 0 }  # If current slot closed, go to first\n                $idx = ($idx + 1) % $openSlots.Count\n                $currentSlot = $openSlots[$idx]\n                $s = Load-Shader $currentSlot\n                Load-TerminalEffects $currentSlot\n                $dirty = $false\n            }\n        }\n        # Enter key (VK 13) to launch windows (only if launchCount > 0)\n        elseif ($vk -eq 13) {\n            if ($launchCount -gt 0) {\n                Save-Shader $currentSlot $s\n                $dirty = $false\n                Launch-MatrixWindows $launchCount\n            }\n        }\n        # Escape key (VK 27) to quit\n        elseif ($vk -eq 27) {\n            # Save current window setup for next Blue Pill run\n            Save-CurrentState\n\n            # Save window positions for exact restoration (like Chrome)\n            $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n            if ($windowInfo.Count -gt 0) {\n                Save-WindowPositions -WindowInfo $windowInfo\n            }\n\n            return\n        }\n        else {\n            # Handle Shift+L (uppercase L) for layout mode BEFORE normalizing\n            # This preserves case-sensitivity for layout toggle vs opacity control\n            if ($k -ceq 'L') {\n                # Cycle layout mode: Pillars -> Quads -> Pillars\n                $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                $newMode = if ($config.Mode -eq 'Pillars') { 'Quads' } else { 'Pillars' }\n                $config.Mode = $newMode\n                Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                Position-MatrixWindows\n                Write-Host \"\"\n                Write-Host \" Layout mode: $newMode\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800\n                continue\n            }\n\n            # Normalize letter keys to lowercase for case-insensitive handling\n            $key = if ($k -match '^[A-Za-z]$') { [char]::ToLower($k) } else { $k }\n\n            switch ($key) {\n                # Color presets (1-6)\n                '1' { $s.R=\"0.0\"; $s.G=\"1.0\"; $s.B=\"0.3\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '2' { $s.R=\"0.0\"; $s.G=\"0.6\"; $s.B=\"1.0\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '3' { $s.R=\"1.0\"; $s.G=\"0.1\"; $s.B=\"0.1\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '4' { $s.R=\"0.7\"; $s.G=\"0.0\"; $s.B=\"1.0\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '5' { $s.R=\"1.0\"; $s.G=\"0.7\"; $s.B=\"0.0\"; $dirty=$true }\n                '6' { $s.R=\"0.0\"; $s.G=\"0.9\"; $s.B=\"0.9\"; $dirty=$true }\n\n                # RGB controls (Q/W, A/S, Z/X)\n                'q' { Adj 'R' -0.05 0 1 }\n                'w' { Adj 'R' 0.05 0 1 }\n                'a' { Adj 'G' -0.05 0 1 }\n                's' { Adj 'G' 0.05 0 1 }\n                'z' { Adj 'B' -0.05 0 1 }\n                'x' { Adj 'B' 0.05 0 1 }\n\n                # Effects (paired keys for -/+)\n                'e' { Adj 'Speed' -0.1 0.1 3 }\n                'r' { Adj 'Speed' 0.1 0.1 3 }\n                'd' { Adj 'Glow' -0.1 0.2 3 }\n                'f' { Adj 'Glow' 0.1 0.2 3 }\n                'c' { Adj 'Width' -1 6 20 }\n                'v' { Adj 'Width' 1 6 20 }\n                't' { Adj 'Trail' -0.5 4 15 }\n                'y' { Adj 'Trail' 0.5 4 15 }\n                'g' { Adj 'Dens' -0.1 0.2 1 }\n                'h' { Adj 'Dens' 0.1 0.2 1 }\n\n                # Layers (7/8/9)\n                '7' { $s.L1 = if($s.L1 -eq \"1.0\"){\"0.0\"}else{\"1.0\"}; $dirty=$true }\n                '8' { $s.L2 = if($s.L2 -eq \"1.0\"){\"0.0\"}else{\"1.0\"}; $dirty=$true }\n                '9' { $s.L3 = if($s.L3 -eq \"1.0\"){\"0.0\"}else{\"1.0\"}; $dirty=$true }\n\n                # Window transparency\n                'b' { $script:transparency = -not $transparency; Apply-WindowTransparency }\n                'k' { if ($transparency -and $opacity -gt 0) { $script:opacity = $opacity - 5; Apply-WindowTransparency } }\n                'l' { if ($transparency -and $opacity -lt 100) { $script:opacity = $opacity + 5; Apply-WindowTransparency } }\n\n                # Launch count controls\n                '-' { if ($launchCount -gt 0) { $script:launchCount = $launchCount - 1 } }\n                '+' {\n                    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n                    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n                    $availableCount = ($existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }).Count\n                    if ($launchCount -lt $availableCount) { $script:launchCount = $launchCount + 1 }\n                }\n                '=' {\n                    $existingSlots = Get-ExistingSlots\n                    $openSlots = Get-OpenMatrixSlots\n                    $availableCount = ($existingSlots | Where-Object { $_ -notin $openSlots }).Count\n                    if ($launchCount -lt $availableCount) { $script:launchCount = $launchCount + 1 }\n                }\n\n                # Reset\n                '0' { $s = $defaults.Clone(); $dirty=$true }\n\n                # Save shader\n                'p' {\n                    Save-Shader $currentSlot $s\n                    $dirty = $false\n                    Write-Host \"\"\n                    Write-Host \" Shader saved! Changes apply automatically.\" -ForegroundColor Green\n                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1200\n                }\n\n                # Windows on Primary controls (< and > keys = comma and period)\n                ',' {\n                    # Decrease windows on primary\n                    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n                    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n                        $current = $config.WindowsOnPrimary\n                        if ($null -eq $current -or $current -lt 0) {\n                            # Auto mode -> switch to one less than total windows\n                            $openCount = (Get-OpenMatrixSlots).Count\n                            $config.WindowsOnPrimary = [Math]::Max(0, $openCount - 1)\n                        } elseif ($current -gt 0) {\n                            $config.WindowsOnPrimary = $current - 1\n                        }\n                        Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                        Position-MatrixWindows\n                        Write-Host \"\"\n                        Write-Host \" Primary: $($config.WindowsOnPrimary) windows\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n                    }\n                }\n                '.' {\n                    # Increase windows on primary\n                    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n                    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n                        $current = $config.WindowsOnPrimary\n                        $openCount = (Get-OpenMatrixSlots).Count\n                        if ($null -eq $current -or $current -lt 0) {\n                            # Already auto (all on primary), can't increase more\n                        } elseif ($current -lt $openCount) {\n                            $config.WindowsOnPrimary = $current + 1\n                            Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                            Position-MatrixWindows\n                            Write-Host \"\"\n                            Write-Host \" Primary: $($config.WindowsOnPrimary) windows\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                ')' {\n                    # Reset to Auto (Shift+0)\n                    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n                    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n                    if ($screens.Count -gt 1) {\n                        $config.WindowsOnPrimary = $null\n                        Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n                        Position-MatrixWindows\n                        Write-Host \"\"\n                        Write-Host \" Primary: Auto (all windows)\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\n                        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n} finally {\n    [Console]::CursorVisible = $true\n}\n","numLines":1055,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1055}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"89c3c926-d653-49d8-9400-ca6cb99f187c"}

Let me create the architecture document for a robust window identification system."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":7,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7558,"cache_read_input_tokens":53576,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7558,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CXHJeZEfX7WjbuwVMxaja","type":"assistant","uuid":"0739ed1f-7897-4d79-a227-42d77ffeb68b","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:40:42.059Z"}
{"parentUuid":"0739ed1f-7897-4d79-a227-42d77ffeb68b","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a9e259c","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_01RB6su9PzKoJvS3LHWbhcw1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012gihpn6cpGT5HeSpKQ2kWf","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\.claude\\plans\\reactive-snacking-metcalfe-agent-a9e259c.md","content":"# Architecture Plan: Robust Window Identification System\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nCurrent system relies on **window titles** (\"Matrix-1\", \"Matrix-2\") which is fragile:\n- Redpill window has no \"Matrix-\" prefix\n- Titles can change during session\n- No reliable persistence across restarts\n- UI Automation detection is slow (~500ms per window)\n\n**Solution:** Multi-layer identity system combining process tracking, launch-time registration, and persistent registry with fallback hierarchy.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Pattern Analysis - Current System Weaknesses\n\n### Current Identity Methods (from codebase analysis)\n\n**Found in WindowLayoutEngine.ps1, matrix_control.ps1, bluepill.ps1:**\n\n1. **Title Matching** (PRIMARY - BRITTLE)\n   - `FindWindowsByPattern(\"Matrix-\\d+\")` - regex on window title\n   - Location: `matrix_control.ps1:544`, `bluepill.ps1:168`\n   - Issue: Fails for Redpill, fails if title changes\n\n2. **UI Automation** (SECONDARY - SLOW)\n   - `Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation($windowHandle)` - walks UI tree to find \"Matrix-N\" in TermControl\n   - Location: `bluepill.ps1:133-150`, `matrix_control.ps1` (removed in favor of title matching)\n   - Issue: 500ms+ latency per window, unreliable on fast window creation\n\n3. **Window Registry** (TERTIARY - INCOMPLETE)\n   - `window-registry.json`: Maps window handles (as strings) to shader files\n   - Location: `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\window-registry.json`\n   - Example: `\"10160378\": \"Matrix-1.hlsl\"`\n   - Issue: Handles change on window restart, no cleanup mechanism\n\n4. **Process ID + Title Combo** (USED IN FindAllTerminalWindows)\n   - `GetWindowThreadProcessId()` + `Process.GetProcessById()` + process name check\n   - Location: `bluepill.ps1:77-90`, `matrix_control.ps1:305-331`\n   - Issue: Only filters to \"WindowsTerminal\" process, doesn't identify WHICH Matrix window\n\n### Critical Gaps\n\n1. **No launch-time tracking** - when `wt -p \"Matrix-3\"` launches, we don't capture the PID\n2. **No persistent identity** - handle-based registry breaks on restart\n3. **No multi-instance support** - can't distinguish two windows with same profile\n4. **Slow detection** - polling + UI Automation = 500ms+ per window\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: Architecture Design\n\n### Core Principles\n\n1. **Identity Hierarchy**: Multiple ID methods with fallback chain\n2. **Launch-Time Registration**: Capture process info when window is spawned\n3. **Persistent Identity**: Survive window restarts via stable identifiers\n4. **Performance**: <50ms total for all windows, not per-window\n\n### Identity Sources (Priority Order)\n\n#### 1. Launch Tracking (HIGHEST PRIORITY - NEW)\n\n**Concept:** When we launch `wt -p \"Matrix-3\"`, capture the spawned process immediately.\n\n**Data Captured:**\n- Profile name: \"Matrix-3\"\n- Process ID: from `Start-Process -PassThru`\n- Launch timestamp: for correlation\n- Parent GUID: unique ID for this launch session\n\n**Storage:** In-memory hashtable + JSON persistence\n```powershell\n$script:LaunchRegistry = @{\n    \"launch-guid-1234\" = @{\n        ProfileName = \"Matrix-3\"\n        ProcessId = 12345\n        LaunchTime = \"2026-01-19T14:30:00Z\"\n        ShaderFile = \"Matrix-3.hlsl\"\n        CorrelationId = \"launch-guid-1234\"  # Stable across session\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Immediate identification (no polling needed)\n- Works even if title hasn't rendered yet\n- Survives title changes during session\n- Profile name is ground truth (we launched it)\n\n**Limitations:**\n- Doesn't work for windows launched outside our scripts\n- Lost on PowerShell process restart (but persisted to JSON)\n\n#### 2. Process Correlation (HIGH PRIORITY - NEW)\n\n**Concept:** Match running windows to launch records via process tree relationships.\n\n**Windows API:**\n```csharp\n[DllImport(\"ntdll.dll\")]\npublic static extern int NtQueryInformationProcess(\n    IntPtr processHandle,\n    int processInformationClass,\n    ref PROCESS_BASIC_INFORMATION processInformation,\n    int processInformationLength,\n    out int returnLength\n);\n\n[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]\npublic struct PROCESS_BASIC_INFORMATION {\n    public IntPtr Reserved1;\n    public IntPtr PebBaseAddress;\n    public IntPtr Reserved2_0;\n    public IntPtr Reserved2_1;\n    public IntPtr UniqueProcessId;\n    public IntPtr InheritedFromUniqueProcessId;  // Parent PID\n}\n```\n\n**Algorithm:**\n1. EnumWindows to find all WindowsTerminal.exe windows\n2. For each window, get PID via `GetWindowThreadProcessId()`\n3. Query parent PID via `NtQueryInformationProcess()`\n4. Match against `$LaunchRegistry` by PID or parent PID\n5. If match found, use profile name from launch record\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Correlates windows to launch records reliably\n- Doesn't depend on window title\n- Fast (single API call per window)\n\n**Limitations:**\n- Requires NT APIs (not in standard P/Invoke)\n- May fail if parent process exits\n\n#### 3. Command Line Arguments (MEDIUM PRIORITY - NEW)\n\n**Concept:** Read the command line used to launch the process to extract profile name.\n\n**Windows API:**\n```csharp\n[DllImport(\"kernel32.dll\")]\npublic static extern IntPtr OpenProcess(uint dwDesiredAccess, bool bInheritHandle, uint dwProcessId);\n\n[DllImport(\"kernel32.dll\")]\npublic static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr hObject);\n\n// Use WMI or Process.GetProcessById().StartInfo.Arguments (unreliable)\n// OR: Read PEB directly (complex, requires admin)\n```\n\n**PowerShell Alternative (SIMPLER):**\n```powershell\nGet-CimInstance -Class Win32_Process -Filter \"ProcessId = $pid\" | \n    Select-Object -ExpandProperty CommandLine\n```\n\n**Algorithm:**\n1. Get PID from window via `GetWindowThreadProcessId()`\n2. Query WMI/CIM for `CommandLine` property\n3. Parse for `-p \"Matrix-N\"` or `--profile Matrix-N`\n4. Extract profile name\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Works for externally launched windows\n- No dependency on window title\n- Survives window title changes\n\n**Limitations:**\n- WMI queries can be slow (100-200ms)\n- CommandLine may be truncated for long args\n- Requires process to still exist\n\n#### 4. Window Property Store (MEDIUM PRIORITY - NEW)\n\n**Concept:** Use Windows property system to tag windows with custom metadata.\n\n**Windows API:**\n```csharp\n[DllImport(\"shell32.dll\")]\npublic static extern int SHGetPropertyStoreForWindow(\n    IntPtr hwnd,\n    ref Guid riid,\n    out IPropertyStore propertyStore\n);\n\n[ComImport, Guid(\"886D8EEB-8CF2-4446-8D02-CDBA1DBDCF99\")]\npublic interface IPropertyStore {\n    void GetCount(out uint cProps);\n    void GetAt(uint iProp, out PROPERTYKEY pkey);\n    void GetValue(ref PROPERTYKEY key, out PROPVARIANT pv);\n    void SetValue(ref PROPERTYKEY key, ref PROPVARIANT pv);\n    void Commit();\n}\n```\n\n**Algorithm:**\n1. When window is detected, tag it with custom property:\n   - `PKEY_AppUserModel_ID` or custom GUID\n   - Value: \"MatrixShader:Matrix-3:launch-guid-1234\"\n2. On subsequent detections, read property to identify window\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Persistent across title changes\n- Fast retrieval (native window property)\n- Standardized Windows mechanism\n\n**Limitations:**\n- Requires COM interop (complex)\n- May require elevation\n- Property store may not persist across session\n\n#### 5. Title Matching (LOW PRIORITY - CURRENT FALLBACK)\n\n**Keep existing implementation** for backward compatibility.\n\n**Current code:** `matrix_control.ps1:544`\n```powershell\nif ($win.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n    $slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n    $shaderFile = \"Matrix-$slot.hlsl\"\n}\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Already implemented\n- Works for majority of cases\n- No API calls needed\n\n**Limitations:**\n- Fails for Redpill window\n- Unreliable if user renames profile\n- No uniqueness guarantee\n\n#### 6. UI Automation (LOWEST PRIORITY - EMERGENCY FALLBACK)\n\n**Keep existing implementation** but only use if all other methods fail.\n\n**Current code:** `bluepill.ps1:133-150`\n```powershell\n$auto = [System.Windows.Automation.AutomationElement]\n$winElement = $auto::FromHandle($windowHandle)\n$children = $winElement.FindAll([TreeScope]::Descendants, $condition)\nforeach ($child in $children) {\n    if ($child.Current.Name -match \"^Matrix-(\\d+)$\") {\n        return [int]$Matches[1]\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Can detect profile name from UI tree\n- Works when other methods fail\n\n**Limitations:**\n- 500ms+ latency per window\n- May fail during window initialization\n- Unreliable on fast window creation\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Component Design\n\n### A. WindowIdentityService.ps1 (NEW MODULE)\n\n**Responsibilities:**\n- Centralized window identity management\n- Launch tracking registration\n- Identity resolution with fallback chain\n- Persistent registry management\n\n**Public Functions:**\n\n```powershell\nfunction Register-MatrixWindowLaunch {\n    <#\n    .SYNOPSIS\n        Register a window launch for identity tracking.\n    \n    .DESCRIPTION\n        Called immediately when launching 'wt -p \"Matrix-3\"'.\n        Captures process info and creates correlation record.\n    \n    .PARAMETER ProfileName\n        The profile being launched (e.g., \"Matrix-3\")\n    \n    .PARAMETER ProcessInfo\n        The Process object from Start-Process -PassThru\n    \n    .PARAMETER ShaderFile\n        The shader file associated with this window (e.g., \"Matrix-3.hlsl\")\n    \n    .OUTPUTS\n        CorrelationId (GUID) for tracking this launch\n    \n    .EXAMPLE\n        $proc = Start-Process wt -ArgumentList '-p \"Matrix-3\"' -PassThru\n        $corrId = Register-MatrixWindowLaunch -ProfileName \"Matrix-3\" -ProcessInfo $proc -ShaderFile \"Matrix-3.hlsl\"\n    #>\n    param(\n        [string]$ProfileName,\n        [System.Diagnostics.Process]$ProcessInfo,\n        [string]$ShaderFile\n    )\n    \n    # Implementation: Store in $script:LaunchRegistry + persist to JSON\n}\n\nfunction Resolve-WindowIdentity {\n    <#\n    .SYNOPSIS\n        Resolve window identity using fallback hierarchy.\n    \n    .DESCRIPTION\n        Attempts multiple methods in priority order:\n        1. Launch tracking (via PID correlation)\n        2. Command line parsing (WMI query)\n        3. Title matching (regex)\n        4. UI Automation (slow fallback)\n    \n    .PARAMETER WindowHandle\n        The window handle (IntPtr) to identify\n    \n    .OUTPUTS\n        Hashtable @{ ProfileName, ShaderFile, CorrelationId, IdentitySource, Confidence }\n    \n    .EXAMPLE\n        $identity = Resolve-WindowIdentity -WindowHandle $hwnd\n        # Returns: @{ ProfileName=\"Matrix-3\"; ShaderFile=\"Matrix-3.hlsl\"; IdentitySource=\"LaunchTracking\"; Confidence=100 }\n    #>\n    param(\n        [IntPtr]$WindowHandle\n    )\n    \n    # Implementation: Try each method in order, return first success\n}\n\nfunction Get-AllMatrixWindows {\n    <#\n    .SYNOPSIS\n        Get all Matrix windows with resolved identities.\n    \n    .DESCRIPTION\n        Combines EnumWindows + Resolve-WindowIdentity for all WindowsTerminal windows.\n        Returns comprehensive identity information for each window.\n    \n    .OUTPUTS\n        Array of @{ Handle, ProfileName, ShaderFile, ProcessId, Title, IdentitySource }\n    \n    .EXAMPLE\n        $windows = Get-AllMatrixWindows\n        # Returns array of all Matrix windows with identities\n    #>\n    \n    # Implementation: EnumWindows + Resolve-WindowIdentity for each\n}\n\nfunction Update-WindowIdentityRegistry {\n    <#\n    .SYNOPSIS\n        Persist current window identities to registry JSON.\n    \n    .DESCRIPTION\n        Saves stable identifiers (CorrelationId -> ShaderFile mapping) to disk.\n        Uses atomic write pattern (US-001).\n    \n    .EXAMPLE\n        Update-WindowIdentityRegistry\n    #>\n    \n    # Implementation: Atomic write to window-registry.json\n}\n\nfunction Get-ProcessCommandLine {\n    <#\n    .SYNOPSIS\n        Get command line for a process ID.\n    \n    .DESCRIPTION\n        Uses WMI/CIM to query process command line.\n        Returns null if process doesn't exist or query fails.\n    \n    .PARAMETER ProcessId\n        The process ID to query\n    \n    .OUTPUTS\n        String containing full command line, or $null\n    \n    .EXAMPLE\n        $cmdLine = Get-ProcessCommandLine -ProcessId 12345\n    #>\n    param([uint32]$ProcessId)\n    \n    # Implementation: Get-CimInstance with error handling\n}\n```\n\n**Private Functions:**\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-ProcessIdFromWindowHandle {\n    param([IntPtr]$WindowHandle)\n    # Implementation: GetWindowThreadProcessId() P/Invoke\n}\n\nfunction Get-WindowTitle {\n    param([IntPtr]$WindowHandle)\n    # Implementation: GetWindowText() P/Invoke\n}\n\nfunction Test-LaunchCorrelation {\n    param([uint32]$ProcessId)\n    # Check if PID exists in $script:LaunchRegistry\n}\n\nfunction Parse-ProfileFromCommandLine {\n    param([string]$CommandLine)\n    # Regex to extract \"-p \"Matrix-N\"\" or \"--profile Matrix-N\"\n}\n\nfunction Parse-ProfileFromTitle {\n    param([string]$Title)\n    # Existing regex: \"Matrix-(\\d+)\"\n}\n\nfunction Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation {\n    param([IntPtr]$WindowHandle)\n    # Existing implementation from bluepill.ps1\n}\n```\n\n**Data Structures:**\n\n```powershell\n# In-memory launch registry (module-scoped)\n$script:LaunchRegistry = @{\n    \"correlation-guid-1\" = @{\n        ProfileName = \"Matrix-3\"\n        ProcessId = 12345\n        ParentProcessId = 11000  # PowerShell that launched it\n        LaunchTime = [DateTime]::UtcNow\n        ShaderFile = \"Matrix-3.hlsl\"\n        CorrelationId = \"correlation-guid-1\"\n    }\n}\n\n# Persistent window registry (JSON file)\n# C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\window-identity-registry.json\n{\n    \"version\": \"2.0\",\n    \"windows\": [\n        {\n            \"correlationId\": \"correlation-guid-1\",\n            \"profileName\": \"Matrix-3\",\n            \"shaderFile\": \"Matrix-3.hlsl\",\n            \"lastSeen\": \"2026-01-19T14:30:00Z\",\n            \"processId\": 12345,  # May be stale on restart\n            \"sessionId\": \"session-guid-xyz\"  # Cleared on reboot\n        }\n    ],\n    \"sessions\": {\n        \"session-guid-xyz\": {\n            \"startTime\": \"2026-01-19T14:00:00Z\",\n            \"bootId\": \"boot-20260119-1\"  # Detect reboots\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### B. P/Invoke Additions to WindowLayoutAPI (WindowLayoutEngine.ps1)\n\n**Add to existing WindowLayoutAPI C# class:**\n\n```csharp\n// Process information\n[DllImport(\"kernel32.dll\")]\npublic static extern IntPtr OpenProcess(uint dwDesiredAccess, bool bInheritHandle, uint dwProcessId);\n\n[DllImport(\"kernel32.dll\")]\npublic static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr hObject);\n\npublic const uint PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION = 0x0400;\npublic const uint PROCESS_VM_READ = 0x0010;\n\n// Get process parent (requires NT API - OPTIONAL, complex)\n// Fallback: Use WMI Win32_Process.ParentProcessId property instead\n```\n\n### C. Integration Points\n\n**1. matrix_control.ps1 Integration:**\n\n```powershell\n# At script startup (line ~117, after importing WindowLayoutEngine)\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowIdentityService.ps1\"\n\n# Replace Get-MatrixWindowInfo function (line 531-579)\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowInfo {\n    # Use WindowIdentityService instead of title matching\n    return Get-AllMatrixWindows\n}\n\n# In Launch-MatrixWindows function (line 670-682)\nforeach ($slot in $slotsToLaunch) {\n    $pname = \"Matrix-$slot\"\n    Write-Host \"   Waiting for $pname...\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray -NoNewline\n    \n    # START CHANGE: Capture process info\n    $proc = Start-Process wt -ArgumentList \"-p `\"$pname`\"\" -PassThru\n    $corrId = Register-MatrixWindowLaunch -ProfileName $pname -ProcessInfo $proc -ShaderFile \"Matrix-$slot.hlsl\"\n    # END CHANGE\n    \n    if (Wait-ForMatrixWindow $pname) {\n        Write-Host \" OK\" -ForegroundColor Green\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**2. bluepill.ps1 Integration:**\n\n```powershell\n# At script startup (line ~117, after importing WindowLayoutEngine)\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowIdentityService.ps1\"\n\n# Replace Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill function (line 175-192)\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill {\n    return Get-AllMatrixWindows | Where-Object { $_.ProfileName -notmatch \"Redpill\" }\n}\n\n# In launch loop (line 246-256)\nforeach ($slot in $slots) {\n    $pname = \"Matrix-$slot\"\n    if ($openSlots.ContainsKey($slot)) { continue }\n    \n    # START CHANGE: Capture process info\n    $proc = Start-Process wt -ArgumentList \"-p `\"$pname`\"\" -PassThru\n    Register-MatrixWindowLaunch -ProfileName $pname -ProcessInfo $proc -ShaderFile \"Matrix-$slot.hlsl\"\n    # END CHANGE\n    \n    if (Wait-ForMatrixWindow $pname) {\n        Write-Host \" OK\" -ForegroundColor Green\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**3. WindowLayoutEngine.ps1 Integration:**\n\n```powershell\n# Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout function (line 997-1138)\n# Change: Accept ProfileName in WindowHandles hashtable\n# Before: @{ \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = $hwnd } }\n# After:  @{ \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = $hwnd; ProfileName = \"Matrix-1\"; CorrelationId = \"guid\" } }\n\n# This allows layout engine to log which profile is being positioned\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: Implementation Plan\n\n### Step 1: Create WindowIdentityService.ps1 (NEW FILE)\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Create `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowIdentityService.ps1`\n2. Implement P/Invoke declarations:\n   - `GetWindowThreadProcessId` (copy from existing code)\n   - `GetWindowText` (copy from existing code)\n   - `OpenProcess` + `CloseHandle` (new)\n3. Implement core functions:\n   - `Register-MatrixWindowLaunch`\n   - `Get-ProcessIdFromWindowHandle`\n   - `Get-ProcessCommandLine` (using WMI)\n   - `Parse-ProfileFromCommandLine`\n   - `Parse-ProfileFromTitle` (copy existing regex)\n   - `Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation` (copy from bluepill.ps1)\n4. Implement `Resolve-WindowIdentity` with fallback chain:\n   ```powershell\n   # Priority 1: Launch tracking\n   $pid = Get-ProcessIdFromWindowHandle $WindowHandle\n   if (Test-LaunchCorrelation $pid) {\n       return $script:LaunchRegistry[$corrId]\n   }\n   \n   # Priority 2: Command line parsing\n   $cmdLine = Get-ProcessCommandLine $pid\n   $profile = Parse-ProfileFromCommandLine $cmdLine\n   if ($profile) { return @{ ProfileName = $profile; IdentitySource = \"CommandLine\" } }\n   \n   # Priority 3: Title matching\n   $title = Get-WindowTitle $WindowHandle\n   $profile = Parse-ProfileFromTitle $title\n   if ($profile) { return @{ ProfileName = $profile; IdentitySource = \"Title\" } }\n   \n   # Priority 4: UI Automation (slow)\n   $profile = Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation $WindowHandle\n   if ($profile) { return @{ ProfileName = $profile; IdentitySource = \"UIAutomation\" } }\n   \n   return $null\n   ```\n5. Implement `Get-AllMatrixWindows`:\n   - EnumWindows to find all WindowsTerminal.exe processes\n   - Filter: process name = \"WindowsTerminal\"\n   - For each, call `Resolve-WindowIdentity`\n   - Return array of identified windows\n6. Implement persistence functions:\n   - `Update-WindowIdentityRegistry` (atomic write to JSON)\n   - `Load-WindowIdentityRegistry` (on module import)\n\n**Files to create:**\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowIdentityService.ps1` (new, ~400 lines)\n\n**Testing:**\n- Unit test each fallback method independently\n- Integration test: launch 3 windows, verify all identified correctly\n- Stress test: launch 6 windows rapidly, verify no missed identities\n\n### Step 2: Integrate with matrix_control.ps1\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Import WindowIdentityService at script startup\n2. Replace `Get-MatrixWindowInfo` to use `Get-AllMatrixWindows`\n3. Update `Launch-MatrixWindows` to call `Register-MatrixWindowLaunch`\n4. Test: Launch windows via Redpill, verify correct identification\n\n**Files to modify:**\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\matrix_control.ps1` (lines ~117, ~531-579, ~670-682)\n\n### Step 3: Integrate with bluepill.ps1\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Import WindowIdentityService at script startup\n2. Replace `Get-MatrixWindowInfoForBluepill` to use `Get-AllMatrixWindows`\n3. Update launch loop to call `Register-MatrixWindowLaunch`\n4. Test: Run bluepill.ps1, verify windows positioned correctly\n\n**Files to modify:**\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\bluepill.ps1` (lines ~117, ~175-192, ~246-256)\n\n### Step 4: Integrate with WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Update `Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout` to accept CorrelationId in WindowHandles\n2. Add logging to show which IdentitySource was used for each window\n3. Test: Position windows, check debug.log for identity sources\n\n**Files to modify:**\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (line ~997-1138)\n\n### Step 5: Update matrix_state.json Schema\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Migrate `window-registry.json` to new `window-identity-registry.json` format\n2. Add schema version field\n3. Add session tracking for reboot detection\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\window-identity-registry.json` (new schema)\n- Migration script: `Migrate-WindowRegistry.ps1`\n\n### Step 6: Testing & Documentation\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Test scenarios:\n   - Launch via Redpill (matrix_control.ps1)\n   - Launch via Blue Pill (bluepill.ps1)\n   - Restart windows (identity persistence)\n   - Reboot system (session invalidation)\n   - External launch (wt -p \"Matrix-3\" from command line)\n2. Performance benchmarks:\n   - Measure identity resolution time for 6 windows\n   - Target: <50ms total for all windows\n3. Update CLAUDE.md with new architecture\n4. Create ARCHITECTURE_WINDOW_IDENTITY.md\n\n**Files to create:**\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\ARCHITECTURE_WINDOW_IDENTITY.md`\n- `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\test-window-identity.ps1`\n\n---\n\n## Phase 5: Edge Cases & Error Handling\n\n### Edge Case 1: Window Launched Externally\n\n**Scenario:** User runs `wt -p \"Matrix-3\"` from command line (not via our scripts).\n\n**Solution:**\n- `Resolve-WindowIdentity` fallback chain will use:\n  1. Command line parsing (detects `-p \"Matrix-3\"`)\n  2. Title matching (detects \"Matrix-3\" in title)\n- Window is identified correctly, but no CorrelationId\n- Layout engine positions it correctly\n\n**Implementation:** Already handled by fallback chain.\n\n### Edge Case 2: Process ID Reuse\n\n**Scenario:** Windows reuses a PID from a closed Matrix window for a new non-Matrix process.\n\n**Solution:**\n- Launch registry entries have timestamp\n- Before correlating by PID, check process name matches \"WindowsTerminal\"\n- If mismatch, invalidate launch registry entry\n\n**Implementation:**\n```powershell\nfunction Test-LaunchCorrelation {\n    param([uint32]$ProcessId)\n    \n    $entry = $script:LaunchRegistry.Values | Where-Object { $_.ProcessId -eq $ProcessId }\n    if (-not $entry) { return $null }\n    \n    # Validate process still exists and is WindowsTerminal\n    try {\n        $proc = Get-Process -Id $ProcessId -ErrorAction Stop\n        if ($proc.ProcessName -ne \"WindowsTerminal\") {\n            # PID reused for different process - invalidate entry\n            Remove-LaunchRegistryEntry $entry.CorrelationId\n            return $null\n        }\n        return $entry.CorrelationId\n    }\n    catch {\n        # Process exited - invalidate entry\n        Remove-LaunchRegistryEntry $entry.CorrelationId\n        return $null\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Edge Case 3: Multiple Windows Same Profile\n\n**Scenario:** User launches two windows with `wt -p \"Matrix-3\"`.\n\n**Solution:**\n- Each launch gets unique CorrelationId\n- Launch registry tracks both separately\n- When identifying windows:\n  - First window matches first CorrelationId (by PID)\n  - Second window matches second CorrelationId (by PID)\n- If PIDs are same (single WT process, multiple tabs):\n  - Fall back to title matching + index order\n  - Assign CorrelationIds sequentially\n\n**Implementation:**\n```powershell\nfunction Register-MatrixWindowLaunch {\n    # Always generate new GUID for CorrelationId\n    $corrId = [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString()\n    \n    $script:LaunchRegistry[$corrId] = @{\n        ProfileName = $ProfileName\n        ProcessId = $ProcessInfo.Id\n        LaunchTime = [DateTime]::UtcNow\n        ShaderFile = $ShaderFile\n        CorrelationId = $corrId\n        InstanceIndex = (Get-InstanceCount $ProfileName)  # 0, 1, 2...\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Edge Case 4: Redpill Window Identification\n\n**Scenario:** Redpill window has title \"RED PILL - Tab 1\", not \"Matrix-N\".\n\n**Solution:**\n- Launch tracking registers profile as \"Redpill\"\n- Title matching uses regex `\"Redpill|RED PILL\"` (case-insensitive)\n- Command line parsing detects `-p \"Redpill\"`\n- Always excluded from layout engine (existing filter)\n\n**Implementation:**\n```powershell\nfunction Parse-ProfileFromTitle {\n    param([string]$Title)\n    \n    # Check for Redpill variations\n    if ($Title -match \"Redpill|RED PILL\") {\n        return \"Redpill\"\n    }\n    \n    # Check for Matrix-N pattern\n    if ($Title -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n        return \"Matrix-$($Matches[1])\"\n    }\n    \n    return $null\n}\n```\n\n### Edge Case 5: System Reboot\n\n**Scenario:** User reboots system. All PIDs invalidated.\n\n**Solution:**\n- On module load, detect reboot via boot ID comparison\n- Clear launch registry (PIDs are stale)\n- Keep persistent registry (CorrelationIds) for position restoration\n- On first window detection post-reboot:\n  - Use command line + title fallbacks\n  - Re-populate launch registry when matches found\n\n**Implementation:**\n```powershell\nfunction Initialize-WindowIdentityService {\n    # Detect reboot\n    $currentBootId = Get-BootId  # Use GetTickCount64 or system uptime\n    $lastBootId = Load-PersistedBootId\n    \n    if ($currentBootId -ne $lastBootId) {\n        Write-Log \"System reboot detected - clearing launch registry\"\n        $script:LaunchRegistry = @{}\n        Save-BootId $currentBootId\n    }\n    else {\n        # Load persisted launch registry (may have valid PIDs)\n        $script:LaunchRegistry = Load-LaunchRegistry\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 6: Performance Optimization\n\n### Target Performance\n\n- **Current:** 500ms+ per window (UI Automation)\n- **Target:** <50ms total for all windows\n\n### Optimization Strategies\n\n1. **Parallel Window Detection:**\n   - EnumWindows gets all handles in single pass (~5ms)\n   - Resolve identity for each window in parallel (PowerShell jobs or runspaces)\n   - Aggregate results\n   - Benefit: 6 windows @ 10ms each = 60ms sequential → 15ms parallel\n\n2. **Cache WMI Queries:**\n   - Query all WindowsTerminal processes at once:\n     ```powershell\n     Get-CimInstance -Class Win32_Process -Filter \"Name = 'WindowsTerminal.exe'\" |\n         Select-Object ProcessId, CommandLine\n     ```\n   - Single WMI query (~100ms) vs 6 individual queries (~600ms)\n   - Build PID→CommandLine hashtable\n   - Use hashtable for lookups (O(1))\n\n3. **Skip UI Automation Unless Necessary:**\n   - Only invoke UI Automation if:\n     - Launch tracking failed\n     - Command line parsing failed\n     - Title matching failed\n   - Expected: 90% of windows identified via faster methods\n\n4. **Lazy Evaluation:**\n   - Don't resolve identity until needed\n   - Cache resolved identities in window handle map\n   - Refresh only when EnumWindows detects new/changed windows\n\n**Implementation:**\n```powershell\nfunction Get-AllMatrixWindows {\n    # Step 1: Get all WindowsTerminal windows (5ms)\n    $windows = [WindowAPI]::FindAllTerminalWindows(\"\")\n    \n    # Step 2: Bulk WMI query for command lines (100ms for all)\n    $cmdLineMap = Get-BulkCommandLines -ProcessName \"WindowsTerminal\"\n    \n    # Step 3: Resolve identities (fast path: launch tracking + command line)\n    $results = @()\n    foreach ($win in $windows) {\n        $pid = Get-ProcessIdFromWindowHandle $win.Key\n        \n        # Try launch tracking (O(1) hashtable lookup)\n        $identity = Test-LaunchCorrelation $pid\n        if ($identity) {\n            $results += @{ Handle = $win.Key; ProfileName = $identity.ProfileName; IdentitySource = \"LaunchTracking\" }\n            continue\n        }\n        \n        # Try command line (O(1) hashtable lookup)\n        $cmdLine = $cmdLineMap[$pid]\n        $profile = Parse-ProfileFromCommandLine $cmdLine\n        if ($profile) {\n            $results += @{ Handle = $win.Key; ProfileName = $profile; IdentitySource = \"CommandLine\" }\n            continue\n        }\n        \n        # Try title (fast regex)\n        $profile = Parse-ProfileFromTitle $win.Value\n        if ($profile) {\n            $results += @{ Handle = $win.Key; ProfileName = $profile; IdentitySource = \"Title\" }\n            continue\n        }\n        \n        # Last resort: UI Automation (slow, rarely hit)\n        $profile = Get-ProfileFromUIAutomation $win.Key\n        if ($profile) {\n            $results += @{ Handle = $win.Key; ProfileName = $profile; IdentitySource = \"UIAutomation\" }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return $results\n}\n\nfunction Get-BulkCommandLines {\n    param([string]$ProcessName)\n    \n    $processes = Get-CimInstance -Class Win32_Process -Filter \"Name = '$ProcessName.exe'\"\n    $map = @{}\n    foreach ($proc in $processes) {\n        $map[[uint32]$proc.ProcessId] = $proc.CommandLine\n    }\n    return $map\n}\n```\n\n**Expected Performance:**\n- EnumWindows: 5ms\n- Bulk WMI query: 100ms (one-time for all processes)\n- Launch tracking lookups: 6 × 1ms = 6ms\n- Command line lookups: 6 × 1ms = 6ms\n- Title regex: 6 × 1ms = 6ms\n- **Total: ~120ms for 6 windows** (vs 3000ms+ with current UI Automation)\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n### Key Innovations\n\n1. **Launch Tracking** - Capture process info at launch time for instant identification\n2. **Fallback Hierarchy** - 4 methods with degrading priority (fast → slow)\n3. **Bulk WMI Queries** - Single query for all processes vs per-window queries\n4. **Persistent Registry** - CorrelationIds survive restarts, positions restored\n5. **Performance** - <50ms target vs 500ms+ current\n\n### API Additions\n\n**WindowIdentityService.ps1 (NEW):**\n- `Register-MatrixWindowLaunch` - Track window launches\n- `Resolve-WindowIdentity` -
